BBEH-Linguini draws on puzzles from the International Linguistics Olympiad, a setting where prior work has shown that explicit verbalization can hurt performance. Hourglass mitigates this tendency, and on Gemini 3.1 Pro, it reverses the effect entirely.
  Ablations confirm that these gains come from the isolation between stages and the quality of the initial induction, not from prompt wording or the particular symbolic form used. It is how information flows through the reasoning process, rather than the language used to express it, that drives inductive reasoning in frozen LLMs.

</details>


### [116] [Playful AI in Professional Email: A Field Experiment on Tone and Recipient Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11749)
*Ziv Ben-Zion,Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过随机交叉现场实验（121名员工，16880封邮件）发现，GPT-5的 playful 语气编辑能提升邮件情感积极性，professional 语气则降低积极性，但两者均未直接影响打开率、回复率或响应时间；然而，发件人自身的积极性显著预测收件人行为，构成AI通过情感语气间接影响工作沟通的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI辅助写作是否以及通过何种渠道改变收件人的实际行为（如打开、回复邮件），填补AI在真实工作场景中因果效应的空白。

Method: 采用随机交叉现场实验设计，121名员工在3周内分别在无辅助、GPT-5 playful编辑、GPT-5 professional编辑三种条件下撰写工作邮件，分析16880封邮件的情绪、打开率、回复率和响应时间。

Result: playful编辑显著提升情绪积极性（B=+0.068, p<0.001），professional编辑显著降低情绪积极性（B=-0.041, p<0.001）；两种编辑均未直接改变打开率、回复率或响应时间；但发件人自身情绪积极性显著预测打开（OR=2.05）和回复（OR=3.32, p<0.001），构成间接影响路径。

Conclusion: AI辅助沟通通过其产生的语言情感基调而非AI的使用本身，间接影响工作场所的参与行为。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly reshaping workplace communication, yet whether AI-assisted writing changes how recipients actually behave, and through what channel, remains unknown. Here, in a randomized crossover field experiment, 121 employees across six companies sent work emails under three conditions over three weeks: unaided writing, GPT-5 rewriting in a playful tone, and GPT-5 rewriting in a professional tone. Across 16,880 emails, playful editing increased emotional positivity (B=+0.068, p<0.001), and professional editing decreased it (B=-0.041, p<0.001), yet neither condition directly altered open rates, reply rates, or response times. Instead, within-sender positivity strongly predicted both opening (OR=2.05) and replying (OR=3.32, p<0.001), a significant indirect pathway through which AI editing shaped behavior, in the absence of any direct effect. These findings suggest that AI-assisted communication shapes workplace engagement not through its use, but through the emotional tone of the language it produces.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Manifold Constrained Conformal Prediction for Spatial Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10008)
*Collin Nill,Trevor Harris,Jason Adams*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的共形预测方法，用于为空间事件集合（如热带气旋生成和地震位置）构建经校准的预测集。该方法将空间点云表示为经验测度，使用（切片）Wasserstein距离进行评分，并通过数据自适应选择准则约束预测集仅支持在训练数据流形附近，同时推导了覆盖率下界。由于预测集解析形式复杂，引入了改进的基于流的采样程序以生成集成预测集。实验表明，该方法在合成数据、热带气旋和地震数据上实现了接近标称的覆盖率，且能量距离和流形距离显著低于最高预测密度区域（HDR）基线和生成模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害预报的不确定性量化对于风险评估至关重要，但现有方法难以有效处理空间事件集合（如点云）的校准预测问题。

Method: 将空间点云表示为经验测度，利用切片Wasserstein距离评分并约束预测集在训练数据流形附近；推导覆盖率下界并通过数据自适应准则优化；引入基于流的采样程序实现预测集的表示与集成。

Result: 在合成数据、热带气旋生成和地震数据上实现接近标称覆盖率，能量距离和流形距离显著低于HDR基线和生成模型基线。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了空间事件集合的共形预测问题，在保证覆盖率的同时显著提升了预测集的质量，适用于自然灾害等空间点云场景。

Abstract: We introduce a new conformal prediction method that constructs calibrated prediction sets over collections of spatial events, such as tropical cyclone genesis and earthquake locations. Forecasting natural hazards has become increasingly important, due to their significant economic impact, and quantifying the uncertainty of predictions is critical for accurate risk assessment. Our approach works by representing spatial point clouds as empirical measures so that we can score them using (sliced) Wasserstein distance, then constraining the resulting distribution-valued prediction set to be supported only near the training data manifold. We derive a coverage lower bound for the intersected sets and show that, in practice, this gap can be made small through a simple data-adaptive selection criterion. Because the resulting set is not analytically tractable, we introduce a modified flow-based sampling procedure, which allows us to represent and apply these prediction sets in practice as ensembles. Numerical experiments on synthetic data, tropical cyclone genesis, and earthquake occurrences show that our method achieves near-nominal coverage, with significantly lower energy distance and manifold distance than highest predictive density region (HDR) baselines along with generative model baselines.

</details>


### [118] [TSCoNet: A Two-Stage Copula CNN-LSTM for Uncertainty-Aware Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10410)
*Jongwook Kim,Jong-Min Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TSCoNet是一种两阶段卷积-循环模型，结合高斯Copula，在保持高精度点预测的同时，为多变量时空环境变量提供校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习模型在环境变量预测上精度高但缺乏不确定性量化；而强制通过最大似然输出不确定性会降低精度，尤其在变量强相关时矛盾更加突出。

Method: 提出TSCoNet，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段学习精确的均值预测；第二阶段固定均值，优化共享表示来估计预测方差，再经标准重校准得到校准的预测区间。

Result: 在模拟非平稳球面空间场和2000-2020年50个城市的月降水与温度真实数据上，TSCoNet匹配了强确定性预测器的精度，同时提供了其无法给出的校准预测区间。

Conclusion: TSCoNet实现了单一工具同时提供高精度点预测和可靠的不确定性量化，解决了多变量时空预测中精度与不确定性估计的矛盾。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of several interrelated environmental variables - such as regional precipitation and temperature, or other correlated geophysical fields - across many locations calls for accurate predictions accompanied by trustworthy statements of their uncertainty. Modern deep-learning models forecast such variables accurately but usually report no uncertainty, and forcing them to output uncertainty through maximum likelihood tends to degrade their accuracy, especially when the variables are strongly correlated. Motivated by this tension, we develop TSCoNet, a two-stage convolutional-recurrent model coupled with a Gaussian copula that jointly forecasts multiple variables over space and time while quantifying predictive uncertainty. The method first learns accurate mean forecasts and then, holding the mean fixed, refines a shared representation to estimate the predictive variance, yielding calibrated prediction intervals after a standard recalibration, so that uncertainty is added without sacrificing point accuracy. We study the approach on simulated non-stationary spatial fields on the sphere and on a real dataset of monthly precipitation and temperature for fifty cities over 2000-2020. The model matches the accuracy of a strong deterministic forecaster while supplying calibrated prediction intervals that the deterministic model cannot, giving a single tool that provides both accurate point forecasts and reliable uncertainty for multivariate spatio-temporal data.

</details>


### [119] [Integrating Background Knowledge for Scalable Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10456)
*Mátyás Schubert,Theofanis Aslanidis,Tom Claassen,Sara Magliacane*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个在因果发现过程中利用背景知识的框架，尤其适用于可扩展的因果发现方法，实证表明该方法能降低计算开销并提升学习结构质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际因果发现应用中常存在专家背景知识，但现有方法大多仅在因果发现后的后处理步骤中使用这些知识，未能充分利用其在降低计算复杂度和提升结构准确性方面的潜力。

Method: 开发了一个在因果发现过程中动态利用背景知识的框架，专注于可扩展的因果发现方法（仅恢复全图的子集），并在多种算法上实现该框架。

Result: 利用背景知识既能降低计算需求，又能提升学习到的因果结构的质量。

Conclusion: 在因果发现过程中（而非后处理）利用背景知识，可以更有效地减少候选因果图空间，提升可扩展性及结构学习精度。

Abstract: Expert background knowledge is often available in practical applications of causal discovery. Such constraints on the true causal graph can help causal discovery in terms of identifiability of causal effects and accuracy of the learned structure, but also in reducing the space of candidate causal graphs. As causal discovery can become computationally expensive for large number of variables, it is crucial to utilize background knowledge effectively during the causal discovery process. However, most current methods only use background knowledge in a postprocessing step after causal discovery to refine the learned graph. In this work, we develop a framework for utilizing background knowledge during the causal discovery process, focusing especially on scalable causal discovery methods that recover only a subset of the whole graph. We implement our framework for multiple algorithms and empirically show that utilizing background knowledge can both reduce computational requirements and increase the quality of the learned structures.

</details>


### [120] [Representation Learning for Semiparametric Causal Mediation Analysis under No Essential Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10540)
*Roberto Faleh,Sofia Morelli,Holger Brandt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出UNIT方法，将深度表示学习与G估计结合，在"无本质异质性"假设下进行结构中介效应估计。第一阶段用TARNet估计异质性处理效应，第二阶段利用该估计作为权重改进结构参数估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中介效应估计方法在存在未测量的中介-结局混杂因素时面临挑战，且传统方法未充分利用处理效应的异质性信息来提高估计效率。

Method: 两阶段估计器：(1) TARNet学习处理组间共享协变量表示，估计条件平均处理效应(CATE)作为异质性依赖的权重函数；(2) 利用该权重代入Zheng和Zhou(2015)的G估计方程，在存在未测量混杂时识别结构参数。

Result: 在非高斯协变量和非线性中介效应的模拟中，TARNet权重将中介系数的第二阶段标准误降低了1.45-1.51倍（中位数，n≥2000），且不增加偏倚或降低覆盖率。

Conclusion: 更精确的第一阶段表示学习能提供更具信息量的权重，从而提升结构参数估计器的精度，在保持无偏性的同时显著提高效率。

Abstract: We propose a two-stage estimator for structural mediation parameters that combines deep representation learning with G-estimation under the "no essential heterogeneity" (NEH) assumption. We call the method UNIT. In the first stage,TARNet estimates the heterogeneous effect of a randomized treatment on a mediator by learning a shared covariate representation across treatment arms.The resulting conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimate provides a plug-in approximation to the heterogeneity-dependent component of the weight function entering the G-estimating equation of Zheng and Zhou (2015), which identifies the structural parameters even in the presence of unmeasured mediator-outcome confounding. We show that more accurate first-stage representation learning can yield a more informative plug-in weight and thereby improve the precision of the structural parameter estimator. In simulations with non-Gaussian covariates and nonlinear mediator effects, TARNet weights reduce the Stage-2 standard error of the mediation coefficient by a factor of $1.45$ to $1.51$ (median across replications, $n \ge 2000$) relative to the classical approach, at no cost to bias or coverage.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond Looking Up, Try Looking Around: Harmonizing Global Structure and Local Consistency in Optimal Transport for Short Text Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10548)
*Zhihao Yao,Yuxuan Gu,Jixuan Yin,Bo Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对现有最优传输（OT）伪标签方法忽略样本间语义一致性的问题，本文提出了一种融合实例级注意力机制与OT的短文本聚类框架CAOT-STC，通过引入邻域语义感知生成可靠伪标签，在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输（OT）的伪标签方法在短文本聚类中忽视了样本间的语义一致性，导致语义相似的样本被分配不同的伪标签，从而产生劣质聚类结果。

Method: (1) 设计实例级注意力机制捕获样本间的语义关系；(2) 将语义关系融入OT公式中，使传输过程具备邻域语义感知能力；(3) 求解改进的OT问题，获得同时考虑样本间语义一致性和样本-聚类全局结构信息的可靠伪标签；(4) 以这些伪标签作为监督信号指导模型训练。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在短文本聚类任务上优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入实例级注意力机制来建模语义一致性，有效改进了OT伪标签的可靠性，从而显著提升短文本聚类性能。

Abstract: Pseudo-labeling based on Optimal Transport (OT) has become an effective mechanism for enhancing short text clustering. Existing OT methods are short in modeling semantic consistencies between samples, which may assign different pseudo-labels to semantically similar samples. These erroneous pseudo-labels can cause the model to produce inferior clusters. This paper proposes a novel short text clustering framework, which remedies the neglect of semantic consistency in existing OT methods, generating reliable pseudo-labels to facilitate clustering. Specifically, the proposed approach first designs an instance-level attention mechanism to capture semantic relationships between samples, which are then integrated into the OT formulation to endow the transport process with neighborhood semantic awareness. By solving the proposed OT formulation, reliable pseudo-labels are obtained that simultaneously account for sample-to-sample semantic consistency and sample-to-cluster global structure information. These pseudo-labels are then used as supervisory signals to guide the model to achieve accurate clustering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at: \href{https://github.com/YZH0905/CAOT-STC}{https://github.com/YZH0905/CAOT-STC}.

</details>


### [122] [Approximation of Analytic Functions by ReLU Neural Networks with Adjustable Depth and Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10589)
*Yanming Lai,Defeng Sun,Yang Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究ReLU网络对解析函数（无限光滑）的逼近，在$(N,L)$-表征下得到了$\mathcal{O}\left(N^{-C L^τ}\right)$的逼近速率，揭示了深度比宽度对解析函数逼近更关键，区别于有限光滑函数类中深度和宽度对称的角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络逼近理论研究大多通过单一参数（如总参数数量）刻画逼近结果，而shen2020deep开创性地使用宽度$N$和深度$L$联合表征逼近速率，提供了更大的架构灵活性。此前基于$(N,L)$-表征的工作聚焦于有限光滑度$s$的函数类，得到对称的$\mathcal{O}\left(N^{-2s/d}L^{-2s/d}\right)$逼近速率。本文旨在填补对解析函数（无限光滑）在这类联合表征下逼近行为的理论空白。

Method: 通过精心的ReLU网络构造来逼近幂函数、多元乘法和多项式，克服光滑参数与逼近精度之间的权衡困难，从而在$(N,L)$-表征下建立解析函数逼近的上界。

Result: 推导出$\mathcal{O}\left(N^{-C L^τ}\right)$的逼近速率，其中$C>0$为常数，$τ>0$受$L$与$N$关系影响；当$N$大致按$L^d$缩放时$τ=1$。结果表明深度比宽度的作用更关键。

Conclusion: 对于解析函数逼近，网络深度相比宽度发挥更重要的作用，这与有限光滑函数类中深度宽度对称的角色形成鲜明对比，揭示了函数光滑性对神经网络架构设计偏好的影响。

Abstract: In contrast to most studies on neural network approximation theory that characterize results through a single parameter, such as the total number of network parameters, \cite{shen2020deep} pioneered the characterization of approximation rates as a joint function of the width parameter $N$ and the depth parameter $L$, thereby granting greater architectural flexibility. Existing works using the $(N,L)$-characterization focus on function classes with finite smoothness $s$, establishing a typical approximation rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(N^{-2s/d}L^{-2s/d}\right)$ with $d$ denoting the input dimension, which indicates that network depth and width play symmetric roles for these classes. In contrast, this paper establishes upper bounds for the approximation of analytic functions, which possess infinite smoothness, via ReLU networks under the $(N,L)$-characterization. Specifically, we derive approximation rates of $\mathcal{O}\left(N^{-C L^τ}\right)$, where $C>0$ is some constant and $τ>0$ is a parameter influenced by the relation between $L$ and $N$. In particular, $τ=1$ if $N$ scales roughly as $L^d$. Our findings reveal that depth plays a more critical role than width in the context of analytic function approximation. The main technical difficulty of obtaining such upper bounds lies in the trade-off between the smoothness parameters and the approximation accuracy. To overcome this difficulty, we employ refined constructions of several ReLU networks to approximate power functions, multivariate multiplication, and polynomials, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [123] [Demixing Sparse Signals from Nonlinear Observations using Generalized Non-convex Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10618)
*Raziyeh Takbiri*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性混合观测下的稀疏向量恢复问题，提出了一种结合Huberized数据保真项与广义折叠凹惩罚（SCAD、MCP）的正则化框架，并设计了带回溯的两块近端交替算法（NLD-PALM）。理论方面，通过精确符号定号分解建立Huber非线性损失的受限强凸性，推导出局部驻点处σ√(s log n/m)的估计误差界、无log n因子的Oracle速率σ√(s/m)，以及未知单调链接函数的共等恢复定理。实验表明该方法在n=512时相比凸ℓ1去混叠和贪婪硬阈值基线更早发生相变，在5%粗大异常值下相比平方损失估计具有35倍精度优势，并能成功实现饱和放大器观测下的尖峰加背景信号去混叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏向量恢复方法主要针对线性观测或已知链接函数的非线性观测，但实际场景中链接函数未知且存在重尾或污染噪声。现有方法在非线性混合观测、未知单调链接以及粗大异常值下的性能有限，缺乏同时处理这些挑战的统一理论框架。

Method: 提出正则化框架：Huberized数据保真项处理重尾污染噪声，广义折叠凹惩罚（SCAD、MCP）实现稀疏性诱导；设计带回溯的NLD-PALM算法，在Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质下保证整个迭代序列收敛到临界点并具备局部线性收敛率。统计理论上，通过精确符号定号分解建立受限强凸性，推导误差界和共等恢复定理。

Result: 估计误差界为σ√(s log n/m)，在beta-min条件下达到无log n因子的Oracle速率σ√(s/m)；未知单调链接函数下实现共等恢复。实验表明：n=512时相变早于凸ℓ1去混叠和贪婪硬阈值基线；5%粗大异常值下精度比平方损失估计高35倍；成功实现饱和放大器观测下的尖峰加背景信号去混叠。

Conclusion: 所提方法在非线性混合稀疏向量恢复中兼具理论保证和实际优越性：无需已知稀疏水平，仅需对称噪声具有有限方差，对重尾和污染噪声鲁棒，且能处理未知单调链接函数。

Abstract: We consider the recovery of a pair of sparse vectors from a limited number of nonlinear observations of their superposition: $y_i=g(\inner{\ba_i}{\bPhi\bw^\ast+\bPsi\bz^\ast})+e_i$, $i=1,\dots,m$, with $m\ll n$, incoherent orthonormal bases $\bPhi,\bPsi$, a scalar link $g$, and noise $e_i$ that may be heavy-tailed or contaminated. We propose a regularization-based framework combining a Huberized data fidelity with generalized folded-concave penalties (SCAD, MCP), and a two-block proximal alternating algorithm with backtracking (NLD-PALM) whose whole iterate sequence provably converges to critical points under the Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz property, with local linear rates. On the statistical side we establish restricted strong convexity of the Huberized nonlinear loss through an exact sign-definite decomposition, and derive estimation error bounds of order $σ\sqrt{s\log(n)/m}$ that hold at \emph{every} localized stationary point, an oracle rate $σ\sqrt{s/m}$ free of $\log n$ and shrinkage bias under a beta-min condition, and a co-equal recovery theorem for \emph{unknown} monotone links via a linear surrogate and a clipped Plan--Vershynin decoupling. The estimator requires no knowledge of the sparsity levels, and its guarantees hold under symmetric noise with only finite variance. Experiments at $n=512$ under a frozen data-driven regularization rule show an earlier phase transition than convex $\ell_1$ demixing and greedy hard-thresholding baselines, a $35\times$ accuracy advantage over squared-loss estimation under $5\%$ gross outliers, and successful demixing of spike-plus-background signals observed through a saturating amplifier.

</details>


### [124] [Edge Cluster Expansion with Radial Rotary Attention for Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10664)
*Zemin Xu,Wenbo Xie,P. Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了SO(2)理论在机器学习原子间势能（MLIPs）中的应用，指出了传统SO(2)线性架构相比SO(3) Clebsch-Gordan张量积（CGTP）的局限性，提出了Wigner D矩阵的直角坐标构造和递归Clebsch-Gordan构造，并引入了两个新的交互构建模块：基于广义非对称收缩的边复合积基和径向旋转复注意力（RRA）。在OMat24、sAlex和MPTrj上训练模型，TECE-OAM-RRA-1.0在Matbench Discovery上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统研究SO(2)理论在MLIPs中的应用，识别传统SO(2)线性架构相对于SO(3) CGTP的局限性，并克服这些限制以提升模型性能。

Method: 提出Wigner D矩阵的直角坐标构造和递归Clebsch-Gordan构造；提出基于广义非对称收缩的边复合积基（Edge Complex Product Basis）和径向旋转复注意力（RRA）；改进了Atomic Cluster Expansion模块。

Result: 在OMat24、sAlex和MPTrj数据集上训练，TECE-OAM-RRA-1.0在Matbench Discovery上取得了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的新交互构建模块和SO(2)理论改进有效提升了MLIPs的性能，达到了SOTA水平。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a systematic investigation of SO(2) theory to machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) and identify the limitations of conventional SO(2) Linear architectures relative to SO(3) Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Products (CGTP). Building on these insights, we propose direct Cartesian construction and recursive Clebsch-Gordan construction of Wigner D-matrices and introduce two novel interaction building blocks. First, we propose the Edge Complex Product Basis based on Generalized Asymmetric Contraction, a new formulation for many-body expansion that directly constructs higher-order interactions on edges through complex-valued equivariant multiplications. Second, we introduce Radial Rotary Complex Attention(RRA), which enhances extrapolation performance and surpasses existing attention vector formulations. We also introduce several improvements to the Atomic Cluster Expansion module. Building on these advances, we train our models on OMat24, sAlex, and MPTrj, and introduce TECE-OAM-RRA-1.0, which achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on the Matbench Discovery.

</details>


### [125] [Long-Memory Reservoir Computing for Data-Scarce Dengue Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11272)
*Rahul Goswami,Shinjini Paul,Palash Ghosh,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了两种结合长记忆特性的储备池计算框架（fESN和wESN），用于解决登革热预测中时间序列短、噪声大、非线性且存在长程依赖的难题。fESN通过分数阶差分嵌入储备池，wESN通过小波平滑提取低频成分，两者均优于统计和深度学习方法，并与共形预测结合提供了校准的不确定性区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 登革热预测对公共卫生规划至关重要，但现有方法面临两难：ARFIMA等统计方法能处理长记忆但线性结构受限，深度神经网络能建模非线性但需大量数据且不显式编码长记忆。标准ESN虽适合小样本非线性建模，但缺乏长程记忆能力。因此需要一种既能捕获非线性动态又具备长记忆特性的小样本预测框架。

Method: 提出长记忆储备池计算框架，包含两个变体：(1) fESN，将分数阶差分动力学融入储备池，直接编码长程依赖；(2) wESN，通过小波平滑提取稳定低频分量，再用记忆感知储备池建模。两者均采用岭回归作为读出层。同时给出了闭环储备池动力学的理论保证，证明标准ESN在温和条件下诱导短记忆过程，而所提方法产生与统计长记忆一致的多项式衰减依赖。

Result: 在多个登革热数据集和不同预测时域上，fESN和wESN均优于统计基线（如ARFIMA）和深度学习基线（如LSTM）。与共形预测结合后，能提供分布自由的校准不确定性区间。

Conclusion: 通过将长记忆机制引入储备池计算，有效解决了小样本非线性时间序列预测中长程依赖建模的难题，为登革热等传染病预测提供了兼具理论保障和实用性能的新范式。

Abstract: Accurate dengue forecasting is crucial for public health planning, but remains challenging because incidence series are often short, noisy, non-stationary, nonlinear, and often affected by long-range temporal dependence. Fractional differencing in Autoregressive Fractionally Integrated Moving Average (ARFIMA) helps balance non-stationarity and persistence, but its linear structure limits its ability to capture nonlinear dynamics. Deep neural networks can model nonlinear patterns, but usually require large training samples and do not explicitly encode statistical long memory. Echo State Networks (ESNs), a widely used reservoir computing framework, are attractive in this setting because they retain nonlinear recurrent dynamics while training only a simple readout, making them suitable for data-scarce scenarios. However, standard ESNs lack long-term memory from a time-series perspective. This study proposes a long-memory reservoir computing framework that integrates dedicated long-memory and short-memory ESN reservoirs with a ridge-regression readout. We introduce two variants: Fractional ESN (fESN), which incorporates fractional-differencing dynamics into the reservoir to encode long-range dependence directly, and Wavelet ESN (wESN), which extracts stable low-frequency components through wavelet smoothing before modeling them with a memory-aware reservoir. We establish theoretical guarantees for closed-loop reservoir dynamics, showing that standard ESNs induce short-memory processes under mild conditions, whereas the proposed long-memory reservoirs generate polynomially decaying dependence consistent with statistical long memory. Across multiple dengue datasets and forecasting horizons, fESN and wESN outperform statistical and deep learning baselines. Combining conformal prediction with fESN and wESN provides distribution-free calibrated uncertainty intervals.

</details>


### [126] [Diversified Multinomial Logit Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11684)
*Heesang Ann,Taehyun Hwang,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出多样化多项逻辑（DMNL）上下文赌博机模型，通过将子模多样性函数融入MNL选择概率，形式化了相关性与多样性的权衡，并设计了避免黑箱优化的白盒UCB算法OFU-DMNL，实现了 $(1-1/(e+1))$ 近似遗憾界 $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{T/K})$ 和高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文MNL赌博机虽能建模相关性驱动选择，但忽略了品类内多样性的潜在收益；而子模/组合赌博机虽编码了多样性奖励，但缺乏结构化的选择概率模型。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，统一建模相关性与多样性。

Method: 提出DMNL上下文赌博机模型，在MNL选择概率中引入一般子模多样性函数；设计白盒UCB算法OFU-DMNL，逐项构造品类并最大化乐观边际增益，避免使用黑箱优化预言机。

Result: OFU-DMNL实现了至少 $(1-1/(e+1))$ 近似遗憾界 $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{T/K})$，优于标准子模基线方法的近似因子；实验表明算法在遗憾上与穷举枚举相当，但运行时间显著降低。

Conclusion: DMNL赌博机为不确定性下的多样性感知品类优化提供了实用基础，OFU-DMNL在统计和计算效率上均具有优势。

Abstract: Existing contextual multinomial logit (MNL) bandits model relevance-driven choice but ignore the potential benefits of within-assortment diversity, while submodular/combinatorial bandits encode diversity in rewards but lack structured choice probabilities. We bridge this gap with the $\textit{diversified multinomial logit}$ (DMNL) contextual bandit, which augments MNL choice probabilities with a generally submodular diversity function, thereby formalizing the relevance--diversity trade-off within a single model. Incorporating diversity renders exact MNL assortment optimization intractable. We propose a $\textit{white-box}$ UCB-based algorithm, $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$, that constructs assortments item-wise by maximizing optimistic marginal gains, avoids black-box optimization oracles. We show that $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$ achieves at least a $(1-\frac{1}{e+1})$-$\textit{approximate}$ regret bound $\tilde{O}\left(d \sqrt{T/K}\right)$, where $d$ is the context dimension, $K$ the maximum assortment size, and $T$ the horizon, and attains an improved approximation factor over standard submodular baselines. Experiments demonstrate consistent gains and, relative to exhaustive enumeration, comparable regret with substantially lower runtime. Overall, DMNL bandits provide a practical foundation for diversity-aware assortment optimization under uncertainty, and $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$ offers a statistically and computationally efficient solution.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [An Occupation-Measure and Frank-Wolfe Framework for Heterogeneous Mean-Field Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09907)
*Di Yu,Sixiong You,Chaoying Pei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种异质性占优测度均值场控制（OM-MFC）框架，将异质性MFC问题转化为测度空间上的凸优化问题，并基于Frank-Wolfe方法实现了可并行分解的求解算法，在无人机协调与搜救场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异质性均值场控制（MFC）公式难以获得可扩展且保持群体结构的求解方法，分析与计算均比同质性情形更困难。

Method: 构建异质性占优测度MFC框架，将问题提升为测度上的群体级优化，约束为测度线性的动力学方程；在正半定矩阵值核条件下证明凸性；提出Frank-Wolfe方法，其线性子问题分解为独立的群体级最优控制问题，无需先验离散化测度空间。

Result: 在无人机协调与搜救数值场景中，该框架能在统一且可计算的轨迹优化框架内捕获对称协调、非对称让行及方向性交互效应。

Conclusion: 所提异质性OM-MFC框架结合Frank-Wolfe方法，为多群体交互控制提供了一种可并行、保结构且计算可扩展的新途径。

Abstract: Heterogeneous mean-field control (MFC) problems involve multiple interacting populations with distinct dynamics, control constraints, and interaction patterns, making both analysis and computation substantially more difficult than in the homogeneous setting. In particular, existing formulations do not readily yield scalable solution methods that preserve population-level structure. To address this, we develop a heterogeneous occupation-measure mean-field control (OM-MFC) framework that lifts the problem to a population-level optimization over measures subject to dynamical constraints that are linear in the measures. We show that the resulting optimization problem is convex under a positive-semidefinite matrix-valued kernel condition, which captures coupled interactions across populations. Based on this formulation, we derive a Frank-Wolfe (FW) method whose linear minimization subproblem decomposes into independent population-wise optimal control problems, enabling parallel computation without requiring an a priori discretization of the measure space. Numerical examples on UAV coordination and search-and-rescue scenarios illustrate that the proposed framework captures symmetric coordination, asymmetric yielding, and directional interaction effects within a unified and computationally tractable trajectory-optimization framework.

</details>


### [128] [Robust Decentralized Optimization under Node Failures via Adaptive Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09939)
*Ilya Kuruzov,Anastasiia Murzina*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对节点可能离开的分布式优化问题，提出Legacy Gradient Tracking (Legacy-GT)方法：离开节点将其函数压缩为梯度锚定二次遗留量并传递给邻居，通过校正精确保持梯度跟踪不变量。理论证明最优遗留曲率为强凸性与光滑性常数的平均值，遗留量可无损组合，自适应锚定规则在最后一次离开后误差几何衰减到残差。而经典丢弃方法存在永不衰减的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法在节点离开时简单丢弃其函数，导致网络剩余节点产生永久性偏差，该偏差与数据的异质性成正比。需要一种机制在节点离开时保留关键信息以消除偏差。

Method: 提出Legacy-GT方法：离开节点将其局部函数压缩为一个梯度锚定的二次函数（遗留量），传递给相邻节点；同时提供校正项以精确保持梯度跟踪不变量。理论分析包括最优遗留曲率选择、遗留量链式组合性质以及自适应锚定规则。

Result: 1) 最优遗留曲率为强凸性常数与光滑性常数的平均值；2) 遗留量可在链式离开中无损组合；3) 自适应锚定规则使误差在最后一次离开后几何衰减到小残差（网络在离开时的优化误差与异质性半径的最小值）；4) 对比方法（drop-and-forget）的偏差永不衰减。

Conclusion: Legacy-GT有效解决了节点离开情况下的分布式优化偏差问题，理论分析和数值实验均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: We study decentralized minimization of a sum of functions over a network where nodes may only leave, the remaining nodes stay connected, and the topology freezes between departures. Standard methods forget departed functions, causing a permanent bias proportional to the heterogeneity of the data. We propose Legacy Gradient Tracking (Legacy-GT): before leaving, a node compresses its function into a gradient-anchored quadratic legacy, bequeaths it to a neighbor, and provides a correction that exactly preserves the gradient-tracking invariant. We prove that the optimal legacy curvature is the average of the strong-convexity and smoothness constants, that legacies compose losslessly across chained departures, and that an adaptive anchor rule yields error bounds that decay geometrically after the last departure to a small residual--the minimum of the network's optimization error at departure and its heterogeneity radius. In contrast, the classic drop-and-forget baseline suffers a bias that never decays. Numerical experiments confirm the theory.

</details>


### [129] [Complexity of partitioned-items response problems: matchings and perfect matchings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09953)
*Christoph Buchheim,Lowig Duer,Eva Ley,Maximilian Merkert,Komal Muluk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究双层优化中的响应问题，探讨领导者如何通过策略引导或强制跟随者构建包含强制项、排除禁止项的可行解，分析了不同问题场景下的计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在双层优化中，领导者如何影响跟随者的决策以包含/排除特定元素，并刻画不同参数组合下的计算可解性边界。

Method: 针对任意底层组合优化问题给出通用结果，再以最大权匹配和最小权完美匹配为具体案例进行分析，结合参数复杂度理论进行分类讨论。

Result: 即使只有一个强制边或禁止边，响应问题也是困难的；但以非强制边总数为参数时是固定参数可解的；当所有边非强制即禁止时，完美匹配的响应问题是多项式可解的，而最大权匹配仍是NP难的。

Conclusion: 响应问题的复杂度高度依赖于强制/禁止项的数量组合以及底层优化问题的结构，揭示了看似相似的匹配问题在双层优化中具有截然不同的计算复杂性。

Abstract: We consider bilevel optimization problems in which leader and follower jointly construct a feasible solution for an underlying combinatorial optimization problem. Response problems ask whether the leader can encourage -- or, in the pessimistic setting, enforce -- a reaction of the follower that includes a set of mandatory items while excluding a set of forbidden items. Our investigation focuses on tractability results for various cases which emerge from different combinations of the total number of mandatory, forbidden, and neutral items. After providing some results for response problems that hold for any underlying combinatorial optimization problem, we examine response problems over the maximum-weight matching problem and the minimum-weight perfect matching problem as illustrative and surprisingly varied examples. Among other results, we show that the response problem is hard for even a single given mandatory or forbidden edge. On the other hand, it is fixed-parameter tractable with respect to the total number of non-mandatory edges. If, however, each follower's edge is either mandatory or forbidden, the response problem for the perfect matching problem is solvable in polynomial time while it remains NP-hard for the maximum-weight matching problem.

</details>


### [130] [Coupled Tensor-Matrix Recovery via Proximal Alternating Linearized Minimization, with an Application to Workforce Skill and Small-Business Health Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10163)
*Analee Miranda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究从稀疏、带噪声的观测中联合恢复一个低秩张量 𝒯 和一个低秩矩阵 M 的问题，两者共享一个模式。通过核范数松弛张量秩，并引入耦合线性算子 G，提出了一个PALM优化算法，证明了其收敛性，推导了样本复杂度理论结果，并在合成数据和两个实际应用场景（ workforce-skill 和 small-business-health 估计）上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从稀疏、带噪声的观测中联合恢复共享一个模式的低秩张量和低秩矩阵的问题，旨在利用耦合关系提升恢复精度，并降低对观测数量的需求。

Method: 采用核范数松弛张量秩（使用 mode-1 展开的核范数），通过学习得到的线性算子 G 耦合张量和矩阵。提出脊正则化惩罚目标函数，并使用近端交替线性化最小化（PALM）算法进行求解。理论方面，证明了目标函数极小值的存在性、PALM算法收敛到临界点、矩阵子问题的采样界，以及顺序子情况下的样本复杂度结果。

Result: 证明了PALM算法收敛到临界点；对于顺序子情况，证明了在耦合算子单独已知时，只需张量的恢复精度即可恢复矩阵 M 而无需其观测数据；在完全联合交替估计的情况下给出了猜想并进行了实验验证；合成实验展示了不同采样密度下的均值和标准差，以及收敛曲线；在 workforce-skill 和 small-business-health 两个应用上进行了框架测试。

Conclusion: 本文提出的耦合低秩张量和矩阵恢复框架在理论和算法上都得到了验证，PALM算法收敛可保证，顺序子情况有严格的样本复杂度理论结果。但在低密度采样区域耦合无助，且应用部分仅为设计方案，尚未在实际部署数据上验证。

Abstract: We study recovery of a low-rank tensor $\mathcal{T}$ and a low-rank matrix $M$ from sparse, noisy observations. $\mathcal{T}$ and $M$ share one mode. We relax tensor rank using the nuclear norm of the mode-1 unfolding. This unfolding carries the coupling. It also has an exact proximal operator. We couple $\mathcal{T}$ and $M$ through a learned linear operator $G$. We prove a minimizer exists for the ridge-stabilized penalized objective. We prove that a proximal alternating linearized minimization (PALM) scheme converges to a critical point, for the algorithm as implemented, by verifying the hypotheses of a known nonconvex block-coordinate convergence theorem against our objective and identifying which conditions come from this problem's structure. For the matrix-only sub-problem, we state a proven sampling bound from matrix completion theory. For the coupled problem, we prove a sample-complexity result for a sequential sub-case: a separately-known coupling operator recovers $M$ from $\mathcal{T}$'s recovery accuracy alone, with no observations of $M$ needed. For the fully joint, alternately-estimated case, we state a conjecture and test it empirically, including a low-density regime where coupling does not help. We report multi-seed synthetic experiments with mean and standard deviation across sampling densities, an asymmetric-density experiment, and convergence curves, and we explain why recovery error stays high at low density. We apply the framework to workforce-skill and small-business-health estimation. Every application-specific choice is a proposed design, not a validated result; we have not run the framework on deployed data.

</details>


### [131] [Exploiting Structure with Anisotropic Consensus-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10205)
*Sabrina Bonandin,Konstantin Riedl,Sara Veneruso*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 各向异性共识优化（CBO）方法能自动检测并利用高维目标函数的可加可分离结构，其计算复杂度仅随目标函数的内在维度 d 呈指数增长，而非环境维度 D，从而有效缓解维数灾难。数值实验验证了理论结果，并指出了未来通过学习对齐噪声与目标结构的改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统各向异性CBO方法在处理高维可加可分离目标函数时，理论上缺乏对其能否缓解维数灾难的严格分析。本文旨在证明该方法在可加可分离结构下的计算复杂度仅依赖于内在维度而非环境维度，从而揭示其有效性并为算法改进提供理论依据。

Method: 通过理论分析证明各向异性CBO的计算复杂度与目标函数的内在维度 d 呈指数关系，而非环境维度 D；同时证明复杂度仅依赖于低维分量的可处理性条件，而非全局能量景观。数值实验验证理论结果，并分析内在维度、可分离程度及分量非凸性对性能的影响。

Result: 证明了各向异性CBO的计算复杂度随内在维度 d 而非环境维度 D 呈指数增长；复杂度仅取决于低维分量的可处理性条件。数值实验证实了理论结果，并表明内在维度、可分离程度和分量复杂度对算法性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: 各向异性CBO能有效处理可加可分离的高维优化问题，前提是各向异性噪声结构与目标函数的可分离结构充分对齐。未来可设计在学习过程中自适应对齐噪声与目标结构以增强算法性能。

Abstract: Anisotropic consensus-based optimization (CBO), a multi-agent metaheuristic derivative-free optimization method, which reliably finds global minima of nonsmooth and nonconvex objective functions while being amenable to rigorous theoretical analysis, automatically detects and exploits additively separable structures of high-dimensional objective functions. This enables the algorithm to mitigate the curse of dimensionality where the objective function decomposes additively into lower-dimensional components. In this paper, we show this property proving that the computational complexity of anisotropic CBO depends exponentially only on the intrinsic dimension $\mathbf{d}$ of the objective function, rather than the ambient dimension $D\gg\mathbf{d}$. Additionally, we demonstrate that the computational complexity depends only on tractability conditions of the lower-dimensional components rather than on the full energy landscape, allowing for a more refined description of the objective function and algorithmic complexity, as the objective function landscape is captured directly at the level of the individual components. Our results highlight the effectiveness of anisotropic CBO for additively separable objective functions provided sufficient alignment between the structure of the anisotropic noise and the separability structure of the objective. This motivates the design of an enhanced algorithm that learns during optimization how to effectively explore the loss landscape by aligning the noise with the structure of the objective function, which we leave for future research. Numerical experiments validate our theoretical results, accentuating the influence of the intrinsic dimensionality $\mathbf{d}$, the level of separability, and the complexity and non-convexity of the objective within the separable components on performance and computational complexity of the anisotropic CBO algorithm.

</details>


### [132] [How much Data do We Need? Sequential Data Collection for Stochastic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10207)
*Xin Li,Juergen Branke,Xuan Vinh Doan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究数据驱动优化中序贯数据收集的最优停止问题，提出基于收益驱动的停止框架，通过贝叶斯学习动态平衡信息增益与采样成本，开发多种停止策略，并在报童问题中验证其能显著减少不必要数据收集的同时实现近乎最优的决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动优化中数据采集往往需要成本，现有方法多假设固定样本量或忽略采样成本，缺乏确定何时停止数据收集的理论指导，导致不必要的数据浪费或决策次优。

Method: 提出收益驱动停止框架：将未知分布参数置于贝叶斯学习框架，序贯更新后验信念；每步评估额外数据的期望边际收益与单位采样成本的关系，决定是否停止；基于该框架开发多种停止策略，并通过报童问题（指数分布需求）进行数值实验，与固定预算和事后最优基准对比。

Result: 收益驱动的停止规则在显著减少不必要数据收集的同时实现了近乎最优的决策性能，优于固定预算策略，与事后最优基准差距很小。

Conclusion: 自适应停止在数据驱动优化中具有有效性，能够平衡信息获取成本与决策质量，为实际序贯采样决策提供了可行方法。

Abstract: Data-driven optimization often requires collecting data to estimate uncertain model parameters before solving the underlying decision problem. In practice, however, data acquisition may incur non-negligible costs, making it critical to determine when to stop additional data collection. In this paper, we study an optimal stopping problem for sequential data collection in stochastic optimization under parameter uncertainty. We propose a benefit-driven stopping framework that balances information gain and sampling cost. We model the unknown distribution parameter within a Bayesian learning framework and update beliefs sequentially as new observations are collected. At each iteration, the decision maker evaluates the expected marginal benefit of additional data relative to the unit sampling cost and determines whether to continue sampling or stop and implement the optimization decision. Based on this framework, we develop several stopping policies. The proposed policies are evaluated through a newsvendor problem with exponentially distributed demand. Numerical experiments compare the policies with fixed-budget and hindsight benchmark strategies. The results show that benefit-driven stopping rules can substantially reduce unnecessary data collection while achieving near-optimal decision performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of adaptive stopping in data-driven optimization.

</details>


### [133] [Neural feedback approximation for stochastic control with degenerate diffusions: error estimates and numerical analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10254)
*Olivier Bokanowski,Jean-François Chassagneux,Marco Scaratti,Xavier Warin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域随机最优控制问题，通过神经网络反馈映射的直接策略学习近似时间离散化格式，证明了时间离散值函数与近似优化神经策略值函数之间平均意义上的定量收敛误差界，该界分离了近最优反馈策略逼近、随机轨迹在紧集上的局部化以及训练优化容差三个误差来源。分析无需转移密度假设，统一涵盖了退化扩散和确定性受控动力学。数值实验验证了方法并分离了时间离散化、分段常数策略限制、神经网络逼近和蒙特卡洛评估等主要误差源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为神经网络策略学习在随机最优控制问题中的应用提供理论收敛保证，并量化各误差源的影响。

Method: 通过直接策略学习（神经网络反馈映射）近似时间离散化随机最优控制问题，推导平均意义下的定量收敛误差界，将误差分解为近最优策略逼近误差、轨迹局部化误差和优化容差三个独立项。

Result: 得到了时间离散值函数与神经网络策略值函数之间的定量收敛估计，误差界由三个可分离的项组成；数值实验在退化随机径向目标问题、HJB benchmark和气藏存储问题上验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法无需转移密度假设，统一适用于退化扩散和确定性系统；数值实验成功分离了时间离散化、分段常数策略限制、神经网络逼近和蒙特卡洛评估等主要误差贡献。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon stochastic optimal control problems and approximate the resulting time-discrete formulation by a direct policy-learning problem over neural-network feedback maps. We prove a quantitative convergence estimate, in an averaged sense, for the error between the time-discrete value and the value induced by an approximately optimized neural policy. The bound separates the approximation of near-optimal feedback policies, the localization of stochastic trajectories on compact sets, and the optimization tolerance in training. The analysis does not require transition-density assumptions and covers possibly degenerate diffusions and deterministic controlled dynamics in a unified framework. Numerical experiments are provided for a degenerate stochastic radial target problem, a Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman benchmark, and a gas storage problem, illustrating the approach and separating the main error sources: time discretization, restriction to piecewise-constant policies, neural-network approximation, and Monte Carlo evaluation.

</details>


### [134] [A Direction Adaptation Evaluation Strategy for Noisy Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10416)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Mahsa Yousefi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方向自适应进化策略（DAES），通过固定自适应矩阵为单位矩阵并采用结构化方向生成机制（对称采样、联合排序选择、三组重组和三角搜索方向），在保持MAES型方法实用性能的同时，实现了非渐近复杂度分析，为非凸、凸和强凸目标函数提供了高概率复杂度界，并在BARON基准问题上与先进求解器MADFO展现了良好的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAES型方法虽然实际性能优异，但缺乏严格的非渐近复杂度理论保证，特别是在含噪导数自由优化场景下，其种群搜索机制与理论分析之间存在鸿沟。

Method: DAES将自适应矩阵固定为单位矩阵，替代矩阵自适应机制；采用沿配对正负方向对称采样，对含噪函数值进行联合排序选择，将重排序后的方向分为三组构造三个重组点，其几何结构定义三角搜索方向；施加带符号充分下降搜索和外推机制。

Result: 为非凸、凸和强凸目标函数建立了高概率复杂度界，在噪声限制精度水平下给出了相应保证；在BARON库655个测试问题上与MADFO对比，展现了评估效率与最终鲁棒性之间的有利平衡。

Conclusion: DAES是首个具有方向自适应结构且具备高概率复杂度保证的含噪MAES型导数自由方法，填补了该类方法在非渐近理论分析方面的空白。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a direction adaptation evolution strategy (DAES) -- a new MAES-type method -- for noisy derivative-free optimization, designed to reconcile the population-based search mechanisms of evolution strategies with rigorous complexity analysis. Unlike standard MAES schemes, DAES fixes the adaptation matrix to the identity and replaces matrix adaptation with a structured direction-generation mechanism based on symmetric sampling, joint sorting-selection of noisy function values, three-group recombination, and a new triangular search direction. Specifically, candidates are sampled along paired positive and negative directions, their inexact function values are jointly ranked, and the reordered directions are partitioned into three groups to construct three recombination points whose geometry defines the triangular direction. A signed sufficient-decrease search and extrapolation mechanism is then applied along this direction. This structure yields a population-based MAES-type algorithm that retains competitive practical behavior while being amenable to nonasymptotic analysis under noisy evaluations. We establish high-probability complexity bounds for nonconvex, convex, and strongly convex objective functions and derive corresponding guarantees at the noise-limited accuracy level. To the best of our knowledge, these results provide the first high-probability complexity guarantees for a noisy MAES-type derivative-free method with this direction-adaptation structure. Finally, numerical experiments on the 655 prince test problems from the BARON collection compare DAES with the advanced MAES-type solver MADFO and show a favorable trade-off between evaluation efficiency and ultimate robustness.

</details>


### [135] [Second order optimality conditions for piecewise regular extremals in Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10434)
*Andrei A. Agrachev,Michele Motta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了积分型最优控制问题中分段正则极值曲线的二阶最优性条件，提出了包含不连续性的Jacobi曲线框架，并给出了弱局部最优性的必要条件与强局部最优性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优控制理论中，二阶最优性条件主要针对光滑极值曲线，但实际控制问题中常出现由有限个正则弧拼接而成的分段正则极值曲线，其切换点处的非光滑性使得经典的共轭点理论不再直接适用，需要发展新的分析框架。

Method: 引入Jacobi曲线（Lagrangian子空间曲线）作为刻画二阶变分的核心对象；对于分段正则极值曲线，允许Jacobi曲线在切换时刻存在间断，建立不连续Jacobi曲线框架；利用该框架推导弱局部最优性的必要条件与强局部最优性的充分条件；以一维自由控制系统为例进行详细说明。

Result: 建立了分段正则情形下Jacobi曲线的完整理论，明确了垂直交点、共轭点与最优性之间的关联关系，给出了有效的二阶最优性检验方法。

Conclusion: 该不连续Jacobi曲线框架成功将二阶最优性条件从光滑极值曲线推广到分段正则极值曲线，为一类常见但此前缺乏系统理论支持的最优控制问题提供了严格的分析工具。

Abstract: We study second-order optimality conditions for optimal control problems with integral cost. We consider extremals obtained by concatenating finitely many regular arcs and prove both a necessary condition for weak local optimality and a sufficient condition for strong local optimality within this class. The key object is the Jacobi curve, a curve of Lagrangian subspaces encoding the second variation along the reference extremal. In the regular case, this curve is smooth and can be identified with the tangent spaces to a suitable field of extremals. For piecewise regular extremals, the field of extremals is only piecewise smooth, and the associated Jacobi curve has discontinuities at the switching times. This makes the relation between vertical intersections, conjugate points, and optimality more delicate. We develop this discontinuous Jacobi-curve framework and show how it yields effective second-order tests. The case of one-dimensional free-control systems is studied in detail as an illustrative class in which piecewise regular extremals arise naturally.

</details>


### [136] [A column generation-based fixed-point heuristic for the service-aware multi-commodity flow problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10459)
*Siv Marie Cartland Hansen,Richard Martin Lusby*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究服务感知多商品流问题（SAMCF），其中需求服从logit选择模型且具有弹性，路由受容量约束。提出一种迭代不动点启发式算法，在2秒内找到近优解，比基准方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有弹性需求和硬容量约束的网络中，运营商需联合决定服务多少需求以及如何路由，现有方法难以高效求解此类非线性问题。

Method: 提出迭代不动点启发式算法，交替求解弹性需求的MCF（通过列生成）和根据服务水平更新需求。同时提出两种线性近似基准方法（分段线性需求函数和McCormick包络）以及用于提供有效下界的分段线性外逼近。

Result: 在公共交通实例上，启发式算法在2秒内找到近优解，速度比基准方法快数个数量级，且在基准方法10分钟内能求解的所有实例上匹配其解质量。

Conclusion: 所提启发式算法在解质量和计算效率上均优于现有基准方法，适用于大规模网络优化问题。

Abstract: We study the Service-Aware Multi-Commodity Flow (SAMCF) problem, in which demand is elastic and governed by a logit choice model while routing is subject to hard capacity constraints. In a centralized, system-optimal setting, the network operator jointly determines how much demand to serve and how to route it. We formulate the SAMCF as a nonlinear program and propose an iterative fixed-point heuristic that alternates between solving an inelastic MCF via column generation and updating demand from the resulting service levels. Two linear approximations based on piecewise-linear demand functions and McCormick envelopes serve as benchmarks, while a piecewise-linear outer-approximation of the demand function is used to provide valid lower bounds. Computational experiments on public transport instances show that the heuristic finds near-optimal solutions in under two seconds - orders of magnitude faster than the benchmark methods - while matching their solution quality on all instances they can solve within a ten-minute time limit.

</details>


### [137] [Optimal class scheduling with minimum distributed vacant seats using binary linear programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10641)
*Ohmchana Klinsod,Parin Chaipunya*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二元线性规划的班级排课数学模型，旨在自动化排课任务并最小化所有教室的剩余座位数。以泰国KMUTT数学系为案例，结果显示该模型相较于人工排课方法有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排课是一项繁琐的任务，传统人工排课效率低下且教室座位利用率不佳，因此需要自动化方法并优化教室资源分配。

Method: 采用二元线性规划（Binary Linear Programming）建立数学模型，以最小化所有教室的剩余座位数为优化目标。

Result: 在泰国KMUTT数学系的案例研究中，该模型比当前人工排课方法表现出显著改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的二元线性规划模型能有效自动化排课任务并提升教室座位利用率。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a mathematical model for class scheduling using binary linear programming with the aims to automate the tedious task and to minimize the leftover seats distributed over all the classrooms. A case study at the Department of Mathematics, King Mongkut's University of Technology Thonburi (KMUTT), Thailand, is also presented. The result from our model is shown to have improved significantly over the current manual scheduling method.

</details>


### [138] [Separation of Regular Co-Radiant Sets via Inner Approximating Sequences, Radial Profile Functions and Applications to Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10727)
*Fernando García-Castaño,Miguel Angel Melguizo-Padial*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于新正则性概念的共辐射集分析框架，通过内逼近序列和径向轮廓函数两个新工具，将经典理论从具有范数基的集合扩展到一般共辐射集，实现了分离定理的推广、近似效率的标量化以及变分不等式中的误差界和精确惩罚原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典共辐射集理论依赖于范数基的存在性，限制了其适用范围。本文旨在建立一个更一般的分析框架，使分离理论和优化方法能够应用于不具范数基的共辐射集，同时为近似效率和变分不等式提供新的理论工具。

Method: 提出了两个新工具：(1) 内逼近序列，用于提供正则性的序列化处理方法并证明一般分离定理；(2) 径向轮廓函数，定义于任意共辐射集上，用于刻画范数基存在性的新特征、在锥固体假设下给出正则性的序列化刻画，并构造标量函数实现近似效率的标量化。

Result: 证明了正则共辐射集的一般分离定理；建立了范数基存在性的新刻画；在相关锥为固体的条件下给出了正则性的序列化表征；实现了无需额外结构假设的近似效率标量化；建立了径向深度和度量误差界，给出了均衡问题和变分不等式中近似径向可行性的充分条件，以及残差零水平集上的精确惩罚原理。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了共辐射集的分离与优化理论，为无范数基的集合提供了分析工具，并建立了从正则性到变分不等式误差界的完整理论链条。

Abstract: We develop a new framework for the analysis of co-radiant sets based on a new notion of regularity. This framework extends the classical theory beyond co-radiant sets admitting a norm-base and provides a unified setting for separation and optimization. We introduce two new tools: inner approximating sequences and the radial profile function. The former provides a sequential approach to regularity and is the key ingredient in the proof of a general separation theorem for regular co-radiant sets, extending the existing separation theory. The latter is defined for arbitrary co-radiant sets and yields new characterizations of the existence of norm-bases, together with sequence-based characterizations of regularity under a solidness assumption on the associated cone. It also gives rise to a scalar function leading to scalarization results for approximate efficiency without additional structural assumptions on the underlying co-radiant set. Finally, we provide a variational interpretation of the separation residual by establishing radial-depth and metric error bounds. These yield sufficient conditions for approximate radial feasibility in equilibrium problems and variational inequalities, and an exact penalization principle on the zero level set of the residual.

</details>


### [139] [Parameter-Free Cubic-Regularized Newton Method: Sharp Complexity and Generalized Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10741)
*Shaoying Fang,Naoki Marumo,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种无参数的三次正则化牛顿法变体，在更弱的广义光滑性条件下，获得了寻找二阶稳定点的最优复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶方法需要事先知道问题依赖的参数，且广义光滑性条件较强。本文希望设计一种无需先验参数的方法，并在更弱的条件下获得更优的复杂度保证。

Method: 提出一种参数无关的三次正则化牛顿法变体，在广义光滑性条件 $\|\nabla^3 f(x)\| \leq L_0 + L_1 \|\nabla f(x)\|$ 下分析；推导了Taylor型不等式并证明其与该广义光滑性条件的等价性。

Result: 获得了寻找 $(\varepsilon, δ)$-二阶稳定点的oracle复杂度上界；当 $L_1=0$ 时，该界在 $L_0$、$\varepsilon$、$\delta$ 和初始函数值差距上的依赖达到最优（除加法对数项外）；改进了现有无参数二阶方法的复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的无参数三次正则化牛顿法在更弱的广义光滑性条件下实现了更优的复杂度，并具有最优性保证。

Abstract: We analyze a variant of the cubic-regularized Newton method for nonconvex optimization. This variant is parameter-free in that it requires no prior knowledge of problem-dependent parameters. Under the generalized smoothness condition $\|\nabla^3 f(x)\| \leq L_0 + L_1 \|\nabla f(x)\|$, we derive an oracle complexity bound for finding an $(\varepsilon, δ)$-second-order stationary point. This assumption is weaker than the generalized smoothness conditions used in existing analyses of second-order methods, while the complexity bound improves upon existing guarantees for parameter-free second-order methods. In particular, when $L_1 = 0$, the bound matches the optimal dependence on $L_0$ as well as on $\varepsilon$, $δ$, and the initial function value gap, up to additive logarithmic terms. To establish this bound, we derive Taylor-type inequalities and prove their equivalence to the generalized smoothness condition.

</details>


### [140] [Enhancing Presolve in Mixed Integer Programming by Combining Probing and Dual Fixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10767)
*Zhao-Wei Wang,Wei-Kun Chen,Yu-Hong Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在混合整数规划求解器中结合探测（probing）与对偶固定（dual fixing）两种预求解技术，提出了将对偶固定嵌入探测框架以及利用探测增强对偶固定的两种方法，在HiGHS求解器上的实验表明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测和对偶固定是MIP求解器中两种强大的预求解技术，但目前尚未充分研究如何将二者有效结合以进一步提升求解性能。

Method: 首先将对偶固定嵌入探测框架中，以导出更多有用的变量蕴含关系；其次开发改进的对偶固定技术，利用探测框架检测更多的变量固定缩减。

Result: 在MIPLIB 2017基准实例上的计算结果表明，所提出的两种结合探测与对偶固定的技术在开源MIP求解器HiGHS上具有潜力。

Conclusion: 将探测与对偶固定相结合可以有效增强MIP预求解的缩减能力，提升求解器性能。

Abstract: Probing and dual fixing are two powerful presolve techniques in mixed integer programming (MIP) solvers. Probing tentatively sets some binary variables to 0 or 1, applies linear constraint based domain propagation techniques to derive better variable bounds, and extracts useful information such as stronger variable implications and better global variable bounds. Dual fixing attempts to fix variables to lower or upper bounds while ensuring that at least one optimal solution is retained, as long as the problem was feasible. In this paper, we investigate how to combine the two approaches in MIP solvers to achieve a better performance. In particular, we first embed dual fixing into the probing framework, deriving more useful variables' implications for enhancing the capability of probing. Then, we develop an improved dual fixing technique where more variable fixings can be applied, and use the probing framework to detect the reductions. Computational results on the MIPLIB 2017 benchmark instances demonstrate the potential of the two proposed techniques in combining probing and dual fixing on the open-source MIP solver HiGHS.

</details>


### [141] [RED-SEGA:Resilient Decentralized Stochastic Proximal Optimization with Gradient Sketching over Time-Varying Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10791)
*Jinhui Hu,Guo Chen,Huaqing Li,Liang Ran,Hexi Liang,Tao Han,Tingwen Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于梯度草图的拜占庭鲁棒去中心化方差缩减算法Gossip-SEGA及其鲁棒扩展RED-SEGA，用于解决数据稀缺但高维的结构风险最小化问题，在时变网络上实现线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒去中心化方差缩减随机梯度算法依赖随机数据采样，在数据稀缺但高维的任务（如图像去模糊）中效率低下，因此需要探索通过梯度草图实现方差缩减的新技术路线。

Method: 将梯度草图技术融入去中心化随机近端梯度下降与gossip通信中，提出Gossip-SEGA算法；为抵御拜占庭攻击，将加权平均替换为范数惩罚近似，得到鲁棒扩展RED-SEGA算法。

Result: 推导了RED-SEGA在时变网络上可靠智能体间达成共识和线性收敛率的充分条件，并通过数值实验验证了算法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于梯度草图的方差缩减方法为拜占庭鲁棒去中心化优化提供了新路径，在数据稀缺高维场景下具有优势，且具备理论保证与实证支撑。

Abstract: Variance reduction is indispensable in Byzantine-resilient decentralized stochastic optimization over multi-agent systems (MASs) for its ability to mitigate gradient noise and thereby enhance the resilient aggregation process. However, most existing Byzantine-resilient decentralized variance-reduced (VR) stochastic gradient algorithms rely on random data sampling, which proves inefficient in data-scarce yet high-dimensional tasks, for instance, image deblurring. This paper pursues an alternative technical line that achieves variance reduction via gradient sketching. To this end, we first formulate a class of structural risk minimization (SRM) problems, where the local objectives are not necessarily decomposable and their gradients may be unavailable. To solve the SRM problems in a decentralized manner, we integrate a gradient-sketching technique into decentralized stochastic proximal gradient descent with gossip communication to propose a decentralized VR stochastic gradient algorithm, dubbed Gossip-SEGA.Since Gossip-SEGA does not provide any resilience against Byzantine attacks, a resilient extension of Gossip-SEGA,namely RED-SEGA,is developed via replacing the weighted average in Gossip-SEGA by a norm-penalized approximation. Theoretically, we derive sufficient conditions for both consensus (among reliable agents) and linear convergence rate of RED-SEGA over time-varying networks. The effectiveness and resilience of the proposed algorithms are validated through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [142] [On the Existence of Almost Periodic Solutions with Applications to Global Entrainment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10838)
*Iasson Karafyllis,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对动力系统中概周期解的存在性与稳定性问题，给出了两个推广结果，并将其应用于非线性时不变控制系统的全局夹带问题。研究表明，无扰动系统的局部指数稳定性与对小输入具有输入-状态稳定性可保证系统对小幅概周期输入的全局夹带，并在Lotka-Volterra系统中得到验证。所有结果可推广至一致回复情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将周期系统的相关结果推广到概周期情形，以研究更广泛的动力系统中概周期解的存在性与稳定性，并为非线性控制系统的全局夹带问题提供理论基础。

Method: 基于局部指数稳定性与输入-状态稳定性（ISS）两个条件，证明系统对小幅概周期输入具有全局夹带性质；通过Lotka-Volterra系统（具有Volterra-Lyapunov稳定交互矩阵）进行实例验证。

Result: 证明了局部指数稳定性与ISS性质可保证全局夹带；在Lotka-Volterra系统中验证了该结论；所有结果可进一步推广到一致回复情形。

Conclusion: 本文为概周期系统的全局夹带问题提供了统一的稳定性分析框架，并为相关生态模型（如Lotka-Volterra）的理论分析提供了工具。

Abstract: This paper provides two results that are useful in the study of the existence and the stability properties of almost periodic solutions for a given dynamical system. The obtained results are generalizations of recent results for periodic systems and are applied to the global entrainment problem in nonlinear time-invariant control systems. It is shown that local exponential stability for the unforced system and input-to-state stability with respect to small inputs can guarantee global entrainment to small almost periodic inputs. In this way, global entrainment is shown in Lotka-Volterra systems with a Volterra-Lyapunov stable interaction matrix. All results can be extended to the uniformly recurrent case.

</details>


### [143] [Optimization on Affine-Transversal Hilbert Submanifolds: Part I -- Theoretical Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10861)
*Yongcun Song,Luhao Xue,Xiaoming Yuan,Hangrui Yue*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了希尔伯特空间中仿射横截子流形上优化问题的理论基础，提出了投影诱导黎曼度量框架，使得欧几里得空间中的标准算法技术可应用于该问题，并给出了黎曼线搜索和信赖域算法的收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为仿射横截希尔伯特子流形（非线性流形与仿射子空间的交集）上的通用优化问题建立严格的理论基础，解决经典黎曼优化中切空间Riesz表示隐式且难以直接计算的问题。

Method: 发展了几何与分析工具（切空间刻画、投影算子、隐式回缩算子）；引入投影诱导黎曼度量，其诱导范数与环境范数一致等价；推导了投影诱导梯度的显式公式，替代了隐式的切空间Riesz表示，构建了可计算的变度量框架。

Result: 投影诱导梯度成为具有显式公式的精确黎曼梯度；在该框架下，欧几里得空间的标准算法可直接应用于仿射横截希尔伯特子流形上的优化，且迭代点严格可行；给出了黎曼线搜索和信赖域算法的收敛性分析。

Conclusion: 该理论框架在保持几何结构（保证收敛分析）的同时，实现了可直接计算的操作符评估，为仿射横截希尔伯特子流形上的算法设计提供了实用的变度量框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the theoretical foundations for the generic optimization problem in a Hilbert space whose feasible set is an affine-transversal Hilbert submanifold given by the intersection of a nonlinear manifold and an affine subspace. We develop the geometric and analytical toolkit, such as the tangent-space characterization, projection operators, and implicit retraction operators, to essentially ensure the feasibility of iterates for algorithmic design. We also derive weak-form expressions for the derivatives of lifted objective functionals. In particular, we introduce a projection-induced Riemannian metric whose induced norm is uniformly equivalent to the ambient norm, under which the projection-induced gradient becomes an exact Riemannian gradient with an explicit formula. This construction replaces the implicit tangent-space Riesz representation underlying classical Riemannian optimization with directly computable operator evaluations, yielding a practical variable-metric framework for algorithmic design while preserving the geometric structure required for convergence analysis. With these theoretical foundations, it becomes possible to apply standard techniques in Euclidean spaces to design algorithms with strictly feasible iterates for the optimization problem on an affine-transversal Hilbert submanifold. We also propose the theoretical frameworks for algorithmic design on affine-transversal Hilbert submanifolds by showcasing the Riemannian line-search and trust-region algorithms with rigorous convergence analysis.

</details>


### [144] [Stochastic Dynamic Barrier Perturbed Gradient Methods for Nonconvex Simple Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10957)
*Mohammad Mahdi Ahmadi,Jincheng Cao,Aryan Mokhtari,Erfan Yazdandoost Hamedani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对随机双层优化中下层约束对偶乘子可能无界的问题，提出SDBPG算法（动态障碍扰动梯度法），通过自适应扰动正则化实现稳定收敛，并给出显式的(ε_f,ε_g)-驻点复杂度保证。进一步提出PR-SDBPG（消除罕见访问假设）和VR-PR-SDBPG（方差缩减改进复杂度），据我们所知，这是首个针对随机非凸-非凸简单双层优化的显式复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机简单双层优化中，下层约束诱导的对偶乘子在下层驻点附近可能无界，导致基于有界对偶的分析失效，并使随机梯度估计不稳定。现有方法缺乏对此问题的显式(ε_f,ε_g)-驻点保证。

Method: 提出SDBPG（随机动态障碍扰动梯度法），一种单循环方法，通过对偶形式的自适应扰动来正则化下层约束的退化性。扰动稳定了乘子，即使在下层驻点区域也能控制偏差和方差。进一步提出PR-SDBPG（惩罚正则化变体，消除罕见访问假设）和VR-PR-SDBPG（利用方差缩减改进样本复杂度）。

Result: 在温和的罕见访问假设下，SDBPG在O(max{ε_f^{-2},ε_g^{-2}})次迭代中找到(ε_f,ε_g)-驻点，上层样本复杂度O(ε^{-4})，下层O(ε^{-6})，其中ε=max{ε_f,ε_g}。PR-SDBPG消除了罕见访问假设，VR-PR-SDBPG通过方差缩减进一步提升样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对随机非凸-非凸简单双层优化问题给出显式(ε_f,ε_g)-驻点复杂度保证的工作。所提方法通过自适应扰动有效克服了对偶乘子无界带来的挑战。

Abstract: We study stochastic simple bilevel optimization with smooth, possibly nonconvex upper- and lower-level objectives accessed only through stochastic gradient oracles. A key challenge is that the dual multiplier induced by the lower-level constraint may become unbounded near lower-level stationary points, invalidating bounded-dual analyses and destabilizing stochastic gradient estimates. To address this, we propose \emph{Stochastic Dynamic Barrier Perturbed Gradient} (SDBPG), a single-loop method that adaptively perturbs the dual formulation to regularize this degeneracy. The perturbation stabilizes the multiplier and yields controlled bias and variance even near the lower-level stationarity region. Under a mild rare-visit assumption, SDBPG finds an $(ε_f,ε_g)$-stationary point in $\mathcal{O}(\max\{ε_f^{-2},ε_g^{-2}\})$ iterations, with sample gradient complexities $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ and $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-6})$ for the upper- and lower-level objectives where $ε=\max\{ε_f,ε_g\}$. We further develop PR-SDBPG, a penalty-regularized variant that eliminates the rare-visit assumption, and VR-PR-SDBPG, which improves the resulting sample complexities entirely through variance reduction. To our knowledge, these are the first explicit $(ε_f,ε_g)$-stationarity guarantees for stochastic nonconvex-nonconvex simple bilevel optimization.

</details>


### [145] [Actor-Critic Learning for Extended Mean Field Control with Deterministic Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11005)
*Ziheng Cheng,Xin Guo,Huyên Pham,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间扩展平均场控制问题，提出了一个无模型强化学习框架，通过确定性反馈策略和策略梯度方法，避免了随机核优化，并设计了基于深度确定性策略梯度的算法，在随机Cucker-Smale一致控制和最优清算等问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展平均场控制方法依赖于模型知识和随机核优化，存在局限性。本文旨在建立一个无模型框架，通过确定性反馈策略直接诱导状态-动作联合分布，从而克服这些限制。

Method: 1) 建立参数化McKean-Vlasov动力学的无模型敏感性公式；2) 导出Wasserstein空间上的优势率函数确定性策略梯度公式；3) 引入依赖于状态、动作和联合分布的值函数与优势率表示；4) 提出基于鞅的学习原理；5) 设计连续时间深度确定性策略梯度算法，结合粒子近似、测度依赖神经网络、时序差分学习以及动作/参数空间探索。

Result: 在随机Cucker-Smale一致控制和含交易拥挤的最优清算问题上，数值实验验证了所提方法的高效性、稳定性和鲁棒性，包括对控制分布显式依赖的问题。

Conclusion: 本文提出的无模型强化学习框架能够有效解决连续时间扩展平均场控制问题，规避了随机核优化的局限，且算法在实际问题中表现稳定高效。

Abstract: This paper develops a model-free reinforcement learning framework for continuous--time extended mean field control problems, where both the dynamics and reward may depend on the joint distribution of states and controls. We adopt deterministic feedback policies, under which the state--action distribution is induced directly as a push--forward of the state law. This avoids optimization over stochastic kernels and bypasses key limitations of existing approaches in extended mean field settings.
  We first establish a model--free sensitivity formula for parameterized McKean--Vlasov dynamics and use it to derive a deterministic policy gradient formula expressed through an advantage--rate function on the Wasserstein space. We then refine this formula by introducing local value and advantage--rate representations that depend on the state, action, and joint state--action distribution, yielding a policy gradient that includes both action derivatives and measure--derivative terms with respect to the control distribution. These characterizations lead to a martingale--based learning principle and motivate a continuous--time deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm combining particle approximations, measure--dependent neural networks, temporal--difference learning, and exploration in either action or parameter space.
  Numerical experiments on stochastic Cucker--Smale consensus control and optimal liquidation with trade crowding demonstrate the efficiency, stability, and robustness of the proposed method, including problems with explicit dependence on the control distribution.

</details>


### [146] [Last-Iterate Convergence of Single-Loop Stochastic Methods for Constrained Convex-Concave Minimax Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11056)
*Taoli Zheng,Jiajin Li,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带约束的光滑凸-凹极小极大优化问题，提出了一种基于扰动框架的随机一阶方法（PS-EG和PS-OGDA），解决了标准随机方差条件下原始方法最后迭代不收敛的难题，并建立了与优化视野相关的O(T^{-1/4})和与优化视野无关的O(T^{-1/5})最后迭代收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的随机外梯度法（S-EG）和随机乐观梯度上升下降法（S-OGDA）在存在随机梯度噪声时，即使在简单的双线性问题上，其最后迭代也可能无法收敛。这一根本性挑战促使研究者寻找能够保证最后迭代收敛的改进方法。

Method: 引入一个简单的扰动框架，将原始凸-凹问题正则化为强凸-强凹问题；然后将S-EG和S-OGDA应用于该扰动问题，得到两个简单的单循环方法PS-EG和PS-OGDA。通过先推导收敛到扰动问题鞍点的平方距离，再将其转化为受限原始-对偶间隙的保证。

Result: 当优化视野已知时，PS-EG和PS-OGDA均达到O(T^{-1/4})的最后迭代收敛率；当优化视野未知时，基于递减扰动和递减步长的anytime变体在一般闭凸可行集上达到O(T^{-1/5})的收敛率；在无约束设置下，PS-EG在梯度范数上达到更优的O(T^{-1/4}) anytime收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提出的扰动框架有效解决了随机一阶方法在凸-凹极小极大优化中最后迭代不收敛的问题，两种方法均能在不同场景下实现可证明的收敛保证，且算法设计简单、为单循环结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we study last-iterate convergence of stochastic first-order methods for constrained smooth convex--concave minimax optimization under the standard bounded-variance stochastic oracle. A fundamental challenge is that the last iterates of vanilla stochastic extragradient (S-EG) and stochastic optimistic gradient descent--ascent (S-OGDA) may fail to converge in the presence of stochastic gradient noise, even for simple bilinear problems. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce a simple perturbation framework that regularizes the original convex--concave problem into a strongly convex--strongly concave one. Applying S-EG and S-OGDA to the perturbed problem yields two simple single-loop methods, referred to as perturbed S-EG (PS-EG) and perturbed S-OGDA (PS-OGDA).
  We establish last-iterate convergence by first deriving convergence in terms of the squared distance to the saddle point of the perturbed problem and then translating this estimate into guarantees for the restricted primal--dual gap. Based on this framework, we establish two types of convergence guarantees. When the optimization horizon is known \emph{a priori}, both PS-EG and PS-OGDA achieve an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ last-iterate convergence rate for the restricted primal--dual gap, which coincides with the standard primal--dual gap on compact feasible domains. When the optimization horizon is unknown, we develop an anytime variant based on diminishing perturbations and diminishing stepsizes. For general closed convex feasible sets, both PS-EG and PS-OGDA achieve an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/5})$ last-iterate convergence rate for the restricted primal--dual gap. Furthermore, in the unconstrained setting, PS-EG admits a sharper $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ anytime convergence rate in terms of the gradient norm.

</details>


### [147] [The optimal rate of convergence in mean field control via recoupled shadow flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11062)
*Sebastian Munoz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明N粒子随机最优控制问题的值函数以经验测度速率一致收敛到平均场控制问题的值函数（d≥3时速率为N^{-1/d}，d=2时为N^{-1/2}√logN），去除了此前所需的半凹性假设，并在一维情形发现经验测度基准并非最优，合作粒子可达到4/7的最优多项式指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Daudin、Delarue和Jackson猜想的收敛速率，去除半凹性假设，并探索一维情形下更优的收敛速率。

Method: 采用控制论方法，从N粒子控制的每个实现构建路径Fokker-Planck流（阴影流），通过最优传输反复与粒子重新耦合，以经验测度速率追踪经验测度。一维情形结合了阴影流的预测校正、实现合作的Gibbs律以及最优性的Schrödinger基态估计。

Result: 建立了平均场成本仅Lipschitz连续（在1-Wasserstein距离下）时值函数的一致收敛率，d≥2时该速率最优；添加公共噪声时以相同速率一致收敛；一维情形下发现合作粒子可达到4/7的指数，优于独立样本的1/2和自由放置点量化的1。

Conclusion: 去除了半凹性假设，证实了猜想的最优速率，并在一维情形揭示了超越经验测度基准的合作粒子最优速率。

Abstract: We prove that the value functions of the $N$-particle stochastic optimal control problem converge uniformly to the value function of the corresponding mean field control problem at the empirical-measure rate ($N^{-1/d}$ for $d\geq3$ and $N^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log N}$ for $d=2$), for mean field costs that are merely Lipschitz continuous in the $1$-Wasserstein distance. For $d\geq2$ the rate is optimal in this class, which covers problems whose mean field optimizers are neither unique nor stable. This proves the rate conjectured by Daudin, Delarue and Jackson, and removes the semiconcavity hypothesis made there. The proof is control-theoretic: from each realization of an $N$-particle control we build a pathwise Fokker--Planck flow -- a shadow flow -- which, repeatedly recoupled to the particles by optimal transport, shadows the empirical measure at the empirical-measure rate. The same rate holds with additive common noise, uniformly in its intensity. Finally, in dimension one we discover that the empirical-measure benchmark is not optimal: cooperating particles beat it, and the optimal polynomial exponent is $4/7$, strictly between the accuracy of independent samples and that of quantization by freely placed points. The proofs combine an anticipating correction of the shadow flow, a Gibbs law implementing the cooperation, and a Schrödinger ground-state estimate for the optimality.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the weak observability of second-order infinite-dimensional evolution systems generated by skew-adjoint operators of the form $iA_0$, where $A_0$ is a self-adjoint elliptic operator. We first establish a spectral characterization of weak observability by introducing the notion of spectral coercivity for the observation operator and proving its equivalence to a suitable resolvent estimate. Our main result reveals a direct link between resolvent estimates for the elliptic operator $A_0$ and the weak observability of the associated evolution generator $A$. More precisely, we prove that a resolvent inequality for $A_0$ implies a Hautus-type spectral observability estimate for $A$, which guarantees the weak observability of the system. This provides a unified spectral framework for weak observability based on the coercivity properties of the observation operator. As an application, we establish explicit weak observability estimates for the wave equation on a rectangular domain under several geometric configurations of the observation region. The analysis combines frequency-domain methods, resolvent estimates, and Fourier analysis, yielding new insights into the interplay between resolvent inequalities, spectral coercivity, and weak observability in infinite-dimensional systems.

</details>

Abstract: We present an algorithm for solving nonlinear least-squares problems subject to a mix of nonlinear and linear constraints. The nonlinear constraints are handled by reformulating the objective as the augmented Lagrangian function while linear constraints are handled directly. Each iteration consists of approximately solving a linearly constrained problem by means of a gradient projection technique. Our approach also involves a structured approximation of the augmented Lagrangian Hessian. We show global convergence of the method and assess the performance through numerical experiments.

Abstract: This paper addresses the optimal control of epidemic dynamics under conflicting socio-economic objectives. We propose an economic Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, applied to an extended SEIR-V (Susceptible-Exposed-Infected-Recovered-Vaccinated) compartmental model to govern the spread of an infectious disease while minimizing economic disruption. The control problem is formulated as a constrained nonlinear optimization problem, in which the controller dynamically adjusts social interaction levels (transmission rate beta) and vaccination efforts to minimize a composite cost function that penalizes fatalities, healthcare capacity violations, and economic losses. We conduct a rigorous sensitivity analysis of the prediction horizon N, demonstrating that the closed loop is robust to the horizon choice and that N = 35 days minimizes the realized cost. Furthermore, both the closed-loop solution and an open-loop turnpike analysis across diverse initial conditions reveal that the celebrated "Hammer and Dance" mitigation strategy emerges naturally as the mathematical optimum: the optimal trajectories anchor to a unique suppression turnpike (maximum lockdown) to drive hospitalizations toward the disease-free equilibrium before progressively reopening the economy. Through a turnpike-based argument we establish practical asymptotic stability of the optimal operating point, providing a mathematically grounded decision-support tool for pandemic policy.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we consider the identification and control of nonlinear systems with finite action spaces. The unknown dynamics are estimated from finite samples with Koopman operator regression in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space, yielding a linear switching predictive model, the switches governed by the value of the control variable. In order to perform control in closed-loop, the learned dynamics are employed in an infinite-horizon optimal control problem with time-varying stage cost, which is solved by means of model predictive control. In a theoretical analysis, we derive learning rates for the Koopman dynamics approximation. We further quantify, under suitable assumptions, the sub-optimality of the model predictive control strategy, both in the case of exact Koopman dynamics, and in the case of learned ones. Numerical simulations on the Duffing oscillator complement our theoretical findings.

Existing models struggle to represent these heterogeneous inputs and rarely explain the causal drivers of consumption. This paper proposes a time-aware tabular deep learning framework for inter-stop energy management. Periodic time encoding is integrated into a parameter-efficient batch-ensemble backbone to jointly learn static and sequential features, while Bayesian optimization with tree-structured density estimation tunes hyperparameters. Causal analysis identifies regenerative braking ratio and average speed as the strongest energy-saving factors, while coasting distance is the main driver of excess consumption. Abstract: We consider constrained optimization problems in which input data are affected by estimation errors. In such settings, Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization provides a principled framework to mitigate model risk by optimizing against worst-case distributions within Wasserstein ambiguity sets. However, the numerical resolution of the resulting problems remains challenging, especially in constrained and combinatorial settings. In this paper, we propose a tractable stochastic approach based on two key ingredients: (i) an entropic regularization of the distributionally robust value function, which makes it possible to compute stochastic gradient estimators, and (ii) the combination of these estimators with a stochastic Frank-Wolfe algorithm, allowing us to optimize the regularized robust objective while naturally handling constraints. We illustrate the method, and its interests against empirical risk minimization, on two classical optimization problems, the minimum quadratic spanning tree and the traffic assignment problems. Our approach provides a general, practical way to address Wasserstein distributionally robust formulations in the presence of constraints.

</details>

Abstract: Due to the nonconvex binary constraints of unit commitment (UC), no uniform linear pricing scheme supports the optimal dispatch. Convex hull pricing (CHP) and copositive duality pricing (CDP) both address this problem. CHP derives the price from the subgradient of the value function of the convex hull relaxation of UC. CDP refers to several different pricing mechanisms that can be constructed from the dual multipliers of the completely positive programming reformulation. In this work, we define a centralized convex hull price over the joint feasible set of UC and prove that, under non-degeneracy, it coincides with the marginal copositive duality price. Numerical experiments on the Scarf example validate this equivalence and quantify the pricing gap introduced by the semidefinite restriction.

Abstract: Two-stage stochastic mixed-integer programs (MIPs) are important tools for decision-making under uncertainty, but representing uncertainty with a large number of scenarios -- while ensuring accurate results -- can make them challenging to solve. Scenario reduction addresses this by finding a distribution supported on fewer scenarios that still yields similar optimal first-stage decisions. In this paper, we revisit the classical scenario reduction theory based on distances between probability distributions and the optimal mass transportation problem. We then review and compare various transportation cost functions from the literature and propose a new one. Using the Forward Selection Algorithm, we prove that our proposed cost function selects the best possible scenario from a given sample on the first draw with respect to the relative approximation error. To reduce the computational cost of evaluating this cost function, we further propose a hybrid algorithm that includes a scenario pre-selection phase. We assess solution quality and computational complexity on the two-stage stochastic unit commitment problem for small 24-bus and large 300-bus case studies. With only around five scenarios, the proposed cost function approximates the full-distribution optimum to within roughly 2.1% and 0.4% error for the small and large cases, respectively. The hybrid algorithm achieves similar solution quality while reducing wall-clock time by a factor of 18 and work (as measured by the Gurobi solver) by a factor of 66 on the large case study.

Abstract: We study the policy convergence of unregularized policy mirror descent (PMD) with arbitrary constant step sizes for finite discounted Markov decision processes. We focus on decomposable mirror maps of the form $h(p)=\sum_a ψ(p(a))$, where $ψ$ satisfies standard Legendre-type assumptions. Under these conditions, we prove that the policy sequence generated by PMD converges in the policy domain to a limiting optimal policy, even when the optimal policy set is not a singleton. This result covers a broad class of commonly used mirror maps, including the squared Euclidean mirror map underlying projected Q-ascent, the negative Shannon entropy underlying softmax natural policy gradient, Tsallis entropy, the Hellinger mapping, and the Fermi-Dirac entropy. Although policy convergence has been established previously for specific PMD instances or for regularized variants such as homotopic PMD, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic and unified policy convergence theory for unregularized PMD under general decomposable mirror maps and arbitrary constant step sizes. Our analysis further reveals that the convergence behavior is governed by the differentiability of $ψ$ at $0$ and $1$, leading to different behaviors, including finite-time termination, asymptotic convergence, and an MDP-dependent dichotomy. When $ψ$ is twice continuously differentiable with strictly positive finite curvature, we further establish local policy convergence rates for the asymptotic convergence cases, covering the standard mirror maps mentioned above.

</details>
